# Gap register (phase 2 page audit)

Generated by `node scripts/audit/build-gap-register.mjs` on 2026-10-17. Do not edit by hand: re-run the script as Round R reviews arrive.

Sources: Round A gap rows in `docs/audit/pages/<account>/*.md`, Round R verdicts and MISSED rows in `*-review.md`.

Status: **OPEN** (confirmed or not yet reviewed), **UNCONFIRMED** (a row or review says it needs a live or database check; still counted as open work), **CLOSED-SINCE** (a review says it is already fixed on main), **WRONG** (a review says the Round A claim is incorrect). Severity is the review's value where a review changed it (see the Severity column notes below the table). Source "A" = Round A, "R" = Round R MISSED row (ID contains R).

Closure statuses (from git history since 2026-10-15, see docs/audit/README.md): **IN-BRANCH** (a commit on an origin/chatgpt/chat-N lane branch cites the ID, not yet on main), **FIXED-ON-MAIN** (a commit on origin/main cites the ID; "Fixed by" is the latest citing commit), **VERIFIED** (FIXED-ON-MAIN plus a test under tests/ or an acceptance report under docs/audit/acceptance/ that names the ID; listed in the notes). A citation overrides OPEN, UNCONFIRMED and CLOSED-SINCE; WRONG stays WRONG. Open work = OPEN + UNCONFIRMED + IN-BRANCH.

Proposed lanes: TPO recruiters, companies, campaigns, inbox-team -> chat-2; TPO drives, drive-workspace, batches, students, requests-verification -> chat-5; TPO home, settings-audit, calendar-reports, college/*, superadmin/* -> chat-9; recruiter, learner, faculty, mentor, public -> chat-7 (chat-4 can take over faculty, chat-11 mentor/projects).
